- **skynet**: Drive SkyNet, the modular nuclear reaction network library of J. Lippuner and L. F. Roberts (ApJS 233, 18 (2017); arXiv:1706.06198; BSD 3-Clause; bitbucket.org/jlippuner/skynet).
